).CALCULATE(
    customer_name=name,
    successful_transactions=COUNT(transactions_made.WHERE(LOWER(status) == 'success')),

).CALCULATE(
    customer_name=customer_name,

[DEBUG] [Q4] Code generated by claude:
# First, calculate the date range for the last 8 weeks excluding current week
# We need to go back 9 weeks and exclude the current week
transactions_info = Transactions.WHERE(
    # Filter for transactions in the last 9 weeks (we'll exclude current week later)
    DATEDIFF("weeks", date_time, DATETIME("now")) <= 8
).CALCULATE(
    # Calculate week offset from now (0 = current week, 1 = last week, etc.)
    weeks_ago = DATEDIFF("weeks", date_time, DATETIME("now")),
    # Determine if transaction happened on weekend (Saturday=7, Sunday=1)
    is_weekend = ISIN(DAYOFWEEK(date_time), (1, 7))
)

# Filter out current week (weeks_ago = 0) and keep only last 8 weeks
last_8_weeks = transactions_info.WHERE(
    (weeks_ago > 0) & (weeks_ago <= 8)
)

# Partition by week and calculate counts
result = last_8_weeks.PARTITION(name="weeks", by=weeks_ago).CALCULATE(
    weeks_ago = weeks_ago,
    total_transactions = COUNT(Transactions),
    weekend_transactions = SUM(Transactions.CALCULATE(is_weekend_int = IFF(is_weekend, 1, 0)).is_weekend_int)
).ORDER_BY(weeks_ago.ASC())

SELECT
  _s0.weeks_ago,
  COALESCE(_s0.agg_0, 0) AS total_transactions,
  COALESCE(_s1.agg_1, 0) AS weekend_transactions
FROM _s0 AS _s0
LEFT JOIN _s1 AS _s1
  ON _s0.weeks_ago = _s1.weeks_ago
ORDER BY
  weeks_ago

# Define the start of the current week and the start of the 8-week period.
start_of_current_week = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')
start_of_period = DATETIME(start_of_current_week, '-8 weeks')

# Filter transactions to include only stocks within the last 8 weeks (excluding the current week).
transactions_in_range = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(ticker.ticker_type) == 'stock') &
    (date_time >= start_of_period) &
    (date_time < start_of_current_week)
)

# Calculate the start date of the week for each transaction and identify if it's a weekend.
transactions_with_week_info = transactions_in_range.CALCULATE(
    week_start_date=DATETIME(date_time, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend=ISIN(DAYNAME(date_time), ("Saturday", "Sunday"))
)

# Partition the transactions by the week's start date and calculate the total and weekend transaction counts.
result = transactions_with_week_info.PARTITION(name="weekly_groups", by=(week_start_date)).CALCULATE(
    week_start_date=week_start_date,
    total_transactions=COUNT(Transactions),
    weekend_transactions=SUM(is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week_start_date.DESC())

    is_weekend = ISIN(DAYOFWEEK(date_time), (1, 7))
)

# Filter out current week (weeks_ago = 0) and keep only last 8 weeks
last_8_weeks = transactions_info.WHERE(
    (weeks_ago > 0) & (weeks_ago <= 8)
)

# Partition by week and calculate counts
result = last_8_weeks.PARTITION(name="weeks", by=weeks_ago).CALCULATE(
    weeks_ago = weeks_ago,
    total_transactions = COUNT(Transactions),
    weekend_transactions = SUM(Transactions.CALCULATE(is_weekend_int = IFF(is_weekend, 1, 0)).is_weekend_int)
).ORDER_BY(weeks_ago.ASC())

  COALESCE(_s1.agg_1, 0) AS weekend_transactions
FROM _s0 AS _s0
LEFT JOIN _s1 AS _s1
  ON _s0.weeks_ago = _s1.weeks_ago
ORDER BY
  weeks_ago

# Define the start of the current week and the start of the 8-week period.
start_of_current_week = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')
start_of_period = DATETIME(start_of_current_week, '-8 weeks')

# Filter transactions to include only stocks within the last 8 weeks (excluding the current week).
stock_transactions_in_period = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(ticker.ticker_type) == 'stock') &
    (date_time >= start_of_period) &
    (date_time < start_of_current_week)
)

# For each transaction, calculate the start of its week and determine if it occurred on a weekend.
transaction_details = stock_transactions_in_period.CALCULATE(
    week_start=DATETIME(date_time, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend=ISIN(DAYNAME(date_time), ("Saturday", "Sunday"))
)

# Group the transactions by the start of the week.
weekly_transaction_groups = transaction_details.PARTITION(name="weeks", by=week_start)

# For each weekly group, count the total transactions and the number of weekend transactions.
result = weekly_transaction_groups.CALCULATE(
    week_start_date=week_start,
    total_transactions=COUNT(Transactions),
    weekend_transactions=SUM(Transactions.is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week_start_date.ASC())

# Get customers who signed up in the last 6 months excluding current month
# First, calculate the date range
recent_customers = Customers.WHERE(
    (join_date >= DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-6 months')) &
    (join_date < DATETIME('now', 'start of month'))
).CALCULATE(
    signup_month = JOIN_STRINGS('-', YEAR(join_date), LPAD(MONTH(join_date), 2, '0'))
)

# Partition by signup month to calculate PMCS and PMAT
result = recent_customers.PARTITION(name='months', by=signup_month).CALCULATE(
    month = signup_month,
    PMCS = COUNT(Customers),
    PMAT = AVG(Customers.transactions_made.amount)
)

# Get customers who signed up in the last 6 months excluding current month
# First, let's calculate the date range
recent_customers = Customers.WHERE(
    (join_date >= DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-6 months')) &
    (join_date < DATETIME('now', 'start of month'))
).CALCULATE(
    signup_month = JOIN_STRINGS('-', YEAR(join_date), LPAD(MONTH(join_date), 2, '0'))
)

# Partition by signup month to calculate PMCS and PMAT
result = recent_customers.PARTITION(name='months', by=signup_month).CALCULATE(
    month = signup_month,
    PMCS = COUNT(Customers),
    PMAT = AVG(Customers.transactions_made.amount)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# First, filter for customers who joined in 2022 and have transactions,
# and create a flag to identify active customers.
customers_filtered = Customers.WHERE(
    (YEAR(join_date) == 2022) & (HAS(transactions_made) == 1)
).CALCULATE(
    country=country,
    is_active_customer=IFF(LOWER(status) == 'active', 1, 0)
)

# Partition the filtered customers by country and calculate the components for the AR.
activity_ratio_by_country = customers_filtered.PARTITION(
    name="by_country", by=(country)
).CALCULATE(
    country=country,
    num_active_customers=SUM(Customers.is_active_customer),
    total_customers_with_transactions=COUNT(Customers)
).CALCULATE(
    country=country,
    AR=IFF(total_customers_with_transactions > 0, (num_active_customers / total_customers_with_transactions) * 100.0, 0)
)

# Filter customers who joined in 2022 and have at least one transaction.
customers_filtered = Customers.WHERE(
    (YEAR(join_date) == 2022) & (HAS(transactions_made) == 1)
)

# Partition the filtered customers by country to calculate metrics for each group.
result = customers_filtered.PARTITION(
    name="country_groups", by=(country)
).CALCULATE(
    # For each country, count the total number of customers and the number of active customers.
    country=country,
    active_customers_count=COUNT(Customers.WHERE(LOWER(status) == 'active')),
    total_customers_count=COUNT(Customers)
).CALCULATE(
    # Calculate the Activity Ratio (AR) and select the final fields.
    # The IFF function prevents division by zero.
    country=country,
    AR=IFF(total_customers_count > 0, (100.0 * active_customers_count) / total_customers_count, 0)
)

# Step 1: Filter transactions to include only 'sell' types from the last month (inclusive).
# Also, extract the ticker symbol to be used as a partition key.
sell_transactions_last_month = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(transaction_type) == 'sell') &
    (date_time >= DATETIME('now', '-1 month'))
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker.symbol
)

# Step 2: Partition the filtered transactions by ticker symbol to group them.
ticker_groups = sell_transactions_last_month.PARTITION(name="tickers", by=(ticker_symbol))

# Step 3: For each ticker group, calculate the total sell amount and total costs (tax + commission).
# Then, use a subsequent CALCULATE to compute the SPM using the provided formula.
result = ticker_groups.CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    total_amount_from_sells=SUM(Transactions.amount),
    total_costs=SUM(Transactions.tax) + SUM(Transactions.commission)
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    spm=IFF(
        total_amount_from_sells > 0,
        (total_amount_from_sells - total_costs) / total_amount_from_sells * 100.0,
        0.0
    )
)

[DEBUG] [Q12] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Filter for 'sell' transactions within the last month and extract the ticker symbol.
# The date filter `date_time >= DATETIME('now', '-1 month')` selects all transactions from exactly one month ago to the present.
# We use LOWER() on transaction_type for a case-insensitive comparison.
transactions_info = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(transaction_type) == 'sell') &
    (date_time >= DATETIME('now', '-1 month'))
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker.symbol
)

# Step 2: Partition the filtered transactions by ticker symbol.
# For each ticker, calculate the total sales amount and the total costs (tax + commission).
ticker_groups = transactions_info.PARTITION(
    name="ticker_groups", by=(ticker_symbol)
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    total_amount_from_sells=SUM(Transactions.amount),
    total_costs=SUM(Transactions.tax) + SUM(Transactions.commission)
)

# Step 3: Calculate the Selling Profit Margin (SPM) for each ticker using the provided formula.
# We use IFF to handle cases where the total sales amount is zero to avoid division-by-zero errors, returning 0.0 in such cases.
# The final result includes only the ticker symbol and the calculated SPM.
result = ticker_groups.CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    spm=IFF(
        total_amount_from_sells > 0,
        (total_amount_from_sells - total_costs) / total_amount_from_sells * 100.0,
        0.0
    )
)

# Step 1: Extract month and symbol from daily prices
price_info = DailyPrices.CALCULATE(
    symbol = ticker.symbol,
    month = JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(date), LPAD(MONTH(date), 2, "0")),
    year = YEAR(date),
    month_num = MONTH(date)
)

# Step 2: Partition by symbol and month to calculate monthly statistics
monthly_stats = price_info.PARTITION(
    name="symbol_month", 
    by=(symbol, month)
).CALCULATE(
    symbol = symbol,
    month = month,
    year = year,
    month_num = month_num,
    avg_close = AVG(DailyPrices.close),
    max_high = MAX(DailyPrices.high),
    min_low = MIN(DailyPrices.low)
)

# Step 3: Calculate MoMC using PREV window function
result = monthly_stats.CALCULATE(
    symbol = symbol,
    month = month,
    avg_close = avg_close,
    max_high = max_high,
    min_low = min_low,
    prev_avg_close = PREV(avg_close, by=(symbol.ASC(), year.ASC(), month_num.ASC()), per="Broker"),
    momc = (avg_close - PREV(avg_close, by=(symbol.ASC(), year.ASC(), month_num.ASC()), per="Broker")) / PREV(avg_close, by=(symbol.ASC(), year.ASC(), month_num.ASC()), per="Broker")
).ORDER_BY(symbol.ASC(), month.ASC())

price_info = DailyPrices.CALCULATE(
    symbol=ticker.symbol,
    month_str=JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(date), LPAD(MONTH(date), 2, "0"))
)

# Step 2: Partition by symbol and month to calculate the monthly aggregate statistics.
monthly_stats = price_info.PARTITION(name="ticker_months", by=(symbol, month_str)).CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month_str,
    avg_close=AVG(DailyPrices.close),
    max_high=MAX(DailyPrices.high),
    min_low=MIN(DailyPrices.low)
)

# Step 3: Partition the monthly statistics by symbol to create groups for the window function.
ticker_groups = monthly_stats.PARTITION(name="tickers", by=symbol)

# Step 4: For each ticker group, calculate the month-over-month change (MoMC) using the PREV window function.
# The PREV function looks at the previous month's average close within the same ticker group.
result = ticker_groups.ticker_months.CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month,
    average_closing_price=avg_close,
    highest_price=max_high,
    lowest_price=min_low,
    prev_month_avg_close=PREV(avg_close, by=month.ASC(), per="tickers")
).CALCULATE(
    symbol,
    month,
    average_closing_price,
    highest_price,
    lowest_price,
    momc=IFF(
        (prev_month_avg_close != None) & (prev_month_avg_close != 0),
        (average_closing_price - prev_month_avg_close) / prev_month_avg_close,
        None
    )
)

price_info = DailyPrices.CALCULATE(
    symbol = ticker.symbol,
    month = JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(date), LPAD(MONTH(date), 2, "0")),
    close = close,
    high = high,
    low = low
)

# Step 2: Partition by symbol and month to calculate monthly statistics
monthly_stats = price_info.PARTITION(
    name="symbol_month", 
    by=(symbol, month)
).CALCULATE(
    symbol = symbol,
    month = month,
    avg_close = AVG(DailyPrices.close),
    max_high = MAX(DailyPrices.high),
    min_low = MIN(DailyPrices.low)
)

# Step 3: Calculate MoMC using PREV to get previous month's average
result = monthly_stats.CALCULATE(
    symbol = symbol,
    month = month,
    avg_close = avg_close,
    max_high = max_high,
    min_low = min_low,
    prev_avg_close = PREV(avg_close, by=(symbol.ASC(), month.ASC()), per="symbol", default=0),
    momc = IFF(
        PREV(avg_close, by=(symbol.ASC(), month.ASC()), per="symbol", default=0) == 0,
        0,
        (avg_close - PREV(avg_close, by=(symbol.ASC(), month.ASC()), per="symbol", default=0)) / PREV(avg_close, by=(symbol.ASC(), month.ASC()), per="symbol", default=0)
    )
).CALCULATE(
    symbol,
    month,
    avg_close,
    max_high,
    min_low,
    momc
).ORDER_BY(symbol.ASC(), month.ASC())

# First, prepare transaction data with customer state and ticker type
transaction_info = Transactions.CALCULATE(
    customer_state = customer.state,
    ticker_type = ticker.ticker_type
)

# Partition by the combination of customer state and ticker type
state_type_combinations = transaction_info.PARTITION(
    name="state_type_groups", 
    by=(customer_state, ticker_type)
).CALCULATE(
    customer_state = customer_state,
    ticker_type = ticker_type,
    num_transactions = COUNT(Transactions)
)

# Get the top 5 combinations by number of transactions
result = state_type_combinations.TOP_K(5, by=num_transactions.DESC())

)

    (YEAR(date_time) == 2023) & 
    (MONTH(date_time) >= 1) & 
    (MONTH(date_time) <= 3) &
    (LOWER(status) == 'success')
).CALCULATE(
    year = YEAR(date_time),
    month = MONTH(date_time)
)

# Partition by year and month, then calculate average price
result = q1_2023_transactions.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_groups", 
    by=(year, month)
).CALCULATE(
    year = year,
    month = month,
    avg_price = AVG(Transactions.price)
).ORDER_BY(year.ASC(), month.ASC())

# Get high-value sales and their payments
high_value_payments = Sales.WHERE(sale_price > 30000).payment.CALCULATE(
    payment_date=payment_date,
    week_start=DATETIME(payment_date, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend=ISIN(DAYOFWEEK(payment_date), (1, 7))  # Sunday=1, Saturday=7
)

# Filter to last 8 weeks excluding current week
current_week_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')
eight_weeks_ago = DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-56 days')  # 8 weeks * 7 days

filtered_payments = high_value_payments.WHERE(
    (week_start >= eight_weeks_ago) & (week_start < current_week_start)
)

# Group by week and count payments
result = filtered_payments.PARTITION(name="weeks", by=week_start).CALCULATE(
    week=week_start,
    total_payments_received=COUNT(payment),
    weekend_payments_received=SUM(payment.is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week.ASC())

# Define the time window boundaries once for efficiency.
time_boundaries = Dealership.CALCULATE(
    start_of_current_week=DATETIME('now', 'start of week'),
    start_of_8_weeks_ago=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-8 weeks')
)

# Filter payments from high-value sales that fall within the last 8 full weeks.
# Also, calculate helper fields for grouping and aggregation.
payments_to_analyze = time_boundaries.PaymentsReceived.WHERE(
    (sale_record.sale_price > 30000) &
    (payment_date >= start_of_8_weeks_ago) &
    (payment_date < start_of_current_week)
).CALCULATE(
    week_start=DATETIME(payment_date, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend=ISIN(DAYNAME(payment_date), ("Saturday", "Sunday"))
)

# Partition the payments by week and calculate the total and weekend payment counts.
weekly_payment_summary = payments_to_analyze.PARTITION(name="weeks", by=(week_start)).CALCULATE(
    week=week_start,
    total_payments_received=COUNT(PaymentsReceived),
    weekend_payments_received=SUM(PaymentsReceived.is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week.ASC())

# Define the date range for the last 8 weeks, excluding the current week.
date_boundaries = Dealership.CALCULATE(
    start_of_current_week=DATETIME('now', 'start of week'),
    start_of_8_weeks_ago=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-8 weeks')
)

payments_in_scope = date_boundaries.PaymentsReceived.WHERE(
    (sale_record.sale_price > 30000) &
    (payment_date >= start_of_8_weeks_ago) &
    (payment_date < start_of_current_week)
).CALCULATE(
    week_start_date=DATETIME(payment_date, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend_payment=ISIN(DAYNAME(payment_date), ("Saturday", "Sunday"))
)

# Partition payments by week and calculate total and weekend payment counts.
result = payments_in_scope.PARTITION(name="weekly_groups", by=week_start_date).CALCULATE(
    week=week_start_date,
    total_payments_received=COUNT(PaymentsReceived),

cars_sold_not_in_inventory = Cars.WHERE(
    # Car must have been sold
    HAS(sale_records) == 1
).WHERE(
    # Check the most recent inventory snapshot shows it's not in inventory
    inventory_snapshots.WHERE(
        RANKING(by=snapshot_date.DESC(), per="Cars", allow_ties=False) == 1
    ).SINGULAR().is_in_inventory == False
)

# Define a contextless expression to get the latest inventory snapshot for each car.
# This uses RANKING to find the snapshot with the most recent date for each car.
latest_inventory_snapshot = Cars.inventory_snapshots.WHERE(
    RANKING(by=snapshot_date.DESC(), per="Cars") == 1
).SINGULAR()

# Filter for cars that have been sold (have sale_records) and are no longer in inventory
# according to their latest snapshot.
sold_and_out_of_stock_cars = Cars.WHERE(
    (HAS(sale_records) == 1) &
    (latest_inventory_snapshot.is_in_inventory == False)
)

# Partition the filtered cars by make and model, then calculate the maximum sale price for each group.
# Finally, order the results by the highest sale price in descending order.
highest_sales_by_make_model = sold_and_out_of_stock_cars.PARTITION(
    name="make_model_groups", by=(make, model)
).CALCULATE(
    make=make,
    model=model,
    highest_sale_price=MAX(Cars.sale_records.sale_price)
).ORDER_BY(highest_sale_price.DESC())

# Get the date range for the last 6 months excluding current month
current_month_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
six_months_ago = DATETIME(current_month_start, '-6 months')

# Filter salespersons hired between 2022 and 2023 and get their sales
eligible_sales = Salespersons.WHERE(
    (YEAR(hire_date) >= 2022) & (YEAR(hire_date) <= 2023)
).sales_made.WHERE(
    (sale_date >= six_months_ago) & (sale_date < current_month_start)
).CALCULATE(
    salesperson_id=salesperson._id,
    salesperson_name=JOIN_STRINGS(' ', salesperson.first_name, salesperson.last_name),
    sale_month=DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of month'),
    sale_price=sale_price
)

# Partition by salesperson and month
result = eligible_sales.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_groups",
    by=(salesperson_id, salesperson_name, sale_month)
).CALCULATE(
    salesperson_id=salesperson_id,
    salesperson_name=salesperson_name,
    month=sale_month,
    pmsps=COUNT(sales_made),  # Count of sales
    pmsr=SUM(sales_made.sale_price)  # Sum of sale prices
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

[DEBUG] [Q39] Code generated by gemini:
# Define the date range at the top level for access throughout the query.
# This establishes the start (6 months ago) and end (current month) of our window.
date_range_def = Dealership.CALCULATE(
    start_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-6 months'),
    end_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
)

# Start with all sales and calculate the month of each sale. This creates a "scaffold"
# of all months that have at least one sale, ensuring we can report on months with
# zero sales for our specific salesperson group.
sales_with_month = date_range_def.Sales.CALCULATE(
    sale_month = DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of month')
)

# Partition all sales by the calculated month. This groups all sales into monthly buckets.
monthly_groups = sales_with_month.PARTITION(name="monthly_sales", by=(sale_month))

# For each month group, calculate the required metrics.
# The filtering for salespersons (hired between 2022-2023) is applied *inside*
# the aggregation functions. This is the key to getting 0s for months that have
# sales, but not from the target salesperson group.
monthly_metrics = monthly_groups.CALCULATE(
    month = sale_month,
    # PMSPS: Count sales where the salesperson was hired in the target years.
    PMSPS = COUNT(Sales.WHERE(
        (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) >= 2022) & (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) <= 2023)
    )),
    # PMSR: Sum sale_price for the same filtered group. Default to 0.0 if no such sales exist.
    PMSR = DEFAULT_TO(SUM(Sales.WHERE(
        (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) >= 2022) & (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) <= 2023)
    ).sale_price), 0.0)
)

# Finally, filter the aggregated monthly results to our desired time window (last 6 months)
# and order them chronologically.
pmsps_and_pmsr_metrics = monthly_metrics.WHERE(
    (month >= start_date) & (month < end_date)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

[DEBUG] [Q39] Code generated by claude:
# Get the last 6 months excluding current month
last_6_months_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-6 months')
current_month_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')

# Filter salespersons hired between 2022 and 2023
eligible_salespersons = Salespersons.WHERE(
    (YEAR(hire_date) >= 2022) & (YEAR(hire_date) <= 2023)
).CALCULATE(
    salesperson_id=_id,
    salesperson_name=JOIN_STRINGS(' ', first_name, last_name)
)

# Get sales data for eligible salespersons in the last 6 months
sales_data = eligible_salespersons.sales_made.WHERE(
    (sale_date >= last_6_months_start) & (sale_date < current_month_start)
).CALCULATE(
    salesperson_id=salesperson_id,
    salesperson_name=salesperson_name,
    sale_month=DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of month'),
    sale_price=sale_price
)

# Partition by salesperson and month to calculate metrics
monthly_metrics = sales_data.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_groups", 
    by=(salesperson_id, salesperson_name, sale_month)
).CALCULATE(
    salesperson_id=salesperson_id,
    salesperson_name=salesperson_name,
    month=sale_month,
    pmsps=COUNT(sales_made),
    pmsr=SUM(sales_made.sale_price)
)

# Generate all possible month-salesperson combinations
all_months = Dealership.CALCULATE(
    month_1=DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-6 months'),
    month_2=DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-5 months'),
    month_3=DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-4 months'),
    month_4=DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-3 months'),
    month_5=DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-2 months'),
    month_6=DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-1 months')
)

# Create all combinations of salespersons and months
all_combinations = eligible_salespersons.CALCULATE(
    salesperson_id=salesperson_id,
    salesperson_name=salesperson_name,
    month_1=all_months.SINGULAR().month_1,
    month_2=all_months.SINGULAR().month_2,
    month_3=all_months.SINGULAR().month_3,
    month_4=all_months.SINGULAR().month_4,
    month_5=all_months.SINGULAR().month_5,
    month_6=all_months.SINGULAR().month_6
).CALCULATE(
    salesperson_id=salesperson_id,
    salesperson_name=salesperson_name,
    months=[month_1, month_2, month_3, month_4, month_5, month_6]
)

# Final result with all months included
result = all_combinations.CALCULATE(
    salesperson_id=salesperson_id,
    salesperson_name=salesperson_name,
    month=months,
    pmsps=DEFAULT_TO(
        monthly_metrics.WHERE(
            (salesperson_id == salesperson_id) & 
            (month == months)
        ).SINGULAR().pmsps, 
        0
    ),
    pmsr=DEFAULT_TO(
        monthly_metrics.WHERE(
            (salesperson_id == salesperson_id) & 
            (month == months)
        ).SINGULAR().pmsr, 
        0
    )
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC(), salesperson_name.ASC())

# Define the date range for the last 6 months, excluding the current month.
start_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-6 months')
end_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')

# First, create a "scaffold" of all months in the last 6 months that have at least one sale.
# This is done by taking all sales, calculating their month, filtering for the date range,
# and then partitioning by month. This approach ensures that months with zero sales
# from the target group but non-zero sales from other groups are included.
sales_with_month = Sales.CALCULATE(
    sale_month=DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of month')
)

recent_months_scaffold = sales_with_month.WHERE(
    (sale_month >= start_date) & (sale_month < end_date)
).PARTITION(
    name="monthly_groups",
    by=(sale_month)
)

# Now, for each month in our scaffold, calculate the required metrics.
# The metrics are calculated by filtering the 'Sales' sub-collection within each partition
# to only include sales from salespersons hired between 2022 and 2023.
sales_metrics = recent_months_scaffold.CALCULATE(
    month=sale_month,
    # PMSPS: Count sales from the target salespersons for the given month.
    PMSPS=COUNT(
        Sales.WHERE(
            (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) >= 2022) & (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) <= 2023)
        )
    ),
    # PMSR: Sum the sale_price for the same filtered set of sales.
    # Use DEFAULT_TO to ensure months with no relevant sales show 0.0 instead of NULL.
    PMSR=DEFAULT_TO(
        SUM(
            Sales.WHERE(
                (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) >= 2022) & (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) <= 2023)
            ).sale_price
        ),
        0.0
    )
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# The final result is assigned to the variable 'result'.
result = sales_metrics

# First, we define a variable to hold all sales that occurred in the year 2023.
sales_in_2023 = Sales.WHERE(YEAR(sale_date) == 2023)

# Next, we perform a global calculation to get the total revenue and total cost for those sales.
# Total revenue is the sum of 'sale_price' from the filtered sales.
# Total cost is the sum of the 'cost' of the car linked to each sale.
gpm_for_2023 = Dealership.CALCULATE(
    total_revenue=SUM(sales_in_2023.sale_price),
    total_cost=SUM(sales_in_2023.car.cost)
).CALCULATE(
    # We then use the calculated totals to compute the Gross Profit Margin (GPM).
    gpm=(total_revenue - total_cost) / total_cost * 100
)

# Find cars that went out of inventory and were sold on the same day
cars_sold_same_day = Cars.WHERE(
    HAS(inventory_snapshots.WHERE(is_in_inventory == False)) == 1
).CALCULATE(
    car_make = make,
    car_model = model,
    # Get the date when the car went out of inventory (is_in_inventory = False)
    out_of_inventory_date = inventory_snapshots.WHERE(
        is_in_inventory == False
    ).SINGULAR().snapshot_date,
    # Get the sale information
    sale_price = sale_records.SINGULAR().sale_price,
    sale_date = sale_records.SINGULAR().sale_date
).WHERE(
    # Filter for cars where sale date matches the out of inventory date
    sale_date == out_of_inventory_date
)

# Get the car with the highest sale price
result = cars_sold_same_day.TOP_K(1, by=sale_price.DESC()).CALCULATE(
    make = car_make,
    model = car_model,
    sale_price = sale_price
)

[DEBUG] [Q44] Code generated by claude:
# Step 1: Extract year and month from payments and calculate monthly totals
payments_with_month = PaymentsReceived.CALCULATE(
    payment_year = YEAR(payment_date),
    payment_month = MONTH(payment_date)
)

# Step 2: Partition by year and month to get monthly totals
monthly_totals = payments_with_month.PARTITION(
    name="months", 
    by=(payment_year, payment_month)
).CALCULATE(
    year = payment_year,
    month = payment_month,
    total_payments = SUM(PaymentsReceived.payment_amount)
)

# Step 3: Calculate MoM change using PREV window function
result = monthly_totals.CALCULATE(
    year = year,
    month = month,
    total_payments = total_payments,
    prev_month_payments = PREV(total_payments, by=(year.ASC(), month.ASC()), default=0),
    mom_change = total_payments - PREV(total_payments, by=(year.ASC(), month.ASC()), default=0)
).ORDER_BY(year.ASC(), month.ASC())

# Step 1: Extract year and month from each payment date.
payments_by_month = Dealership.PaymentsReceived.CALCULATE(
    payment_year=YEAR(payment_date),
    payment_month=MONTH(payment_date)
)

# Step 2: Group payments by year and month, and calculate the total payment amount for each group.
# The result is ordered by year and month to prepare for the MoM calculation.
monthly_totals = payments_by_month.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_groups",
    by=(payment_year, payment_month)
).CALCULATE(
    payment_year=payment_year,
    payment_month=payment_month,
    total_payments=SUM(PaymentsReceived.payment_amount)
).ORDER_BY(payment_year.ASC(), payment_month.ASC())

# Step 3: Calculate the Month-over-Month (MoM) change using the PREV window function.
# PREV retrieves the total_payments from the previous month based on the specified order.
result = monthly_totals.CALCULATE(
    payment_year=payment_year,
    payment_month=payment_month,
    total_payments=total_payments,
    mom_change=total_payments - PREV(total_payments, by=(payment_year.ASC(), payment_month.ASC()))
)

# Step 1: Extract year and month from payments and calculate monthly totals
payments_with_month = PaymentsReceived.CALCULATE(
    payment_year = YEAR(payment_date),
    payment_month = MONTH(payment_date)
)

# Step 2: Partition by year and month to get monthly totals
monthly_totals = payments_with_month.PARTITION(
    name="months", 
    by=(payment_year, payment_month)
).CALCULATE(
    year = payment_year,
    month = payment_month,
    total_payments = SUM(PaymentsReceived.payment_amount)
)

# Step 3: Calculate MoM change using PREV window function
result = monthly_totals.CALCULATE(
    year = year,
    month = month,
    total_payments = total_payments,
    prev_month_payments = PREV(total_payments, by=(year.ASC(), month.ASC()), default=0),
    mom_change = total_payments - PREV(total_payments, by=(year.ASC(), month.ASC()), default=0)
).ORDER_BY(year.ASC(), month.ASC())

INFO:httpx:HTTP Request: POST https://us-east5-aiplatform.googleapis.com/v1/projects/solid-drive-448717-p8/locations/us-east5/publishers/anthropic/models/claude-opus-4@20250514:streamRawPredict "HTTP/1.1 200 OK"
[DEBUG] [Q60] Code generated by claude:
# Filter payments for the previous ISO week
previous_week_payments = PaymentsReceived.WHERE(
    (payment_date >= DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-7 days')) &
    (payment_date < DATETIME('now', 'start of week'))
).CALCULATE(
    payment_date=payment_date,
    payment_method=payment_method,
    payment_amount=payment_amount
)

# Group by date and payment method, then calculate daily totals
result = previous_week_payments.PARTITION(
    name="daily_method_groups", 
    by=(payment_date, payment_method)
).CALCULATE(
    payment_date=payment_date,
    payment_method=payment_method,
    daily_total=SUM(PaymentsReceived.payment_amount)
).ORDER_BY(payment_date.ASC(), payment_method.ASC())

# Get payments from the previous ISO week
previous_week_payments = PaymentsReceived.WHERE(
    (payment_date >= DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-7 days')) &
    (payment_date < DATETIME('now', 'start of week'))
)

# Partition by date and payment method, then calculate daily totals
result = previous_week_payments.PARTITION(
    name="daily_method_groups", 
    by=(payment_date, payment_method)
).CALCULATE(
    payment_date=payment_date,
    payment_method=payment_method,
    daily_total=SUM(PaymentsReceived.payment_amount)
).ORDER_BY(payment_date.ASC(), payment_method.ASC())

date_info = Dealership.CALCULATE(
    start_of_previous_week=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-7 days'),
    end_of_previous_week=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-1 day')
)

# Filter payments to include only those within the calculated date range
payments_last_week = date_info.PaymentsReceived.WHERE(
    (payment_date >= start_of_previous_week) & (payment_date <= end_of_previous_week)
)

# Partition the filtered payments by date and method, then calculate the total amount for each group
daily_payment_summary = payments_last_week.PARTITION(
    name="payment_groups", 
    by=(payment_date, payment_method)
).CALCULATE(
    payment_day=payment_date,
    payment_type=payment_method,
    total_amount_received=SUM(PaymentsReceived.payment_amount)
)

result = daily_payment_summary

# First, prepare sales data with year, quarter, and customer state
sales_2023 = Sales.WHERE(YEAR(sale_date) == 2023).CALCULATE(
    sale_year = YEAR(sale_date),
    sale_quarter = IFF(MONTH(sale_date) <= 3, 1,
                      IFF(MONTH(sale_date) <= 6, 2,
                          IFF(MONTH(sale_date) <= 9, 3, 4))),
    customer_state = customer.state,
    sale_price = sale_price
)

# Partition by state and quarter to calculate totals
quarterly_sales = sales_2023.PARTITION(
    name="state_quarter_groups", 
    by=(customer_state, sale_quarter)
).CALCULATE(
    state = customer_state,
    quarter = sale_quarter,
    quarter_start_date = IFF(sale_quarter == 1, "2023-01-01",
                            IFF(sale_quarter == 2, "2023-04-01",
                                IFF(sale_quarter == 3, "2023-07-01", "2023-10-01"))),
    total_sales = SUM(Sales.sale_price)
)

# Order results by state and quarter
result = quarterly_sales.ORDER_BY(state.ASC(), quarter.ASC())

Return the month, patient count, and biologic treatment count.Biologic drugs have drug_type = 'biologic'.

[DEBUG] [Q64] Code generated by claude:
# First, let's get treatments from the last 3 months (excluding current month)
# We'll calculate the month for each treatment and filter accordingly
treatments_with_month = Treatments.CALCULATE(
    treatment_month = JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(start_dt), LPAD(MONTH(start_dt), 2, "0")),
    patient_id = patient_id,
    is_biologic = LOWER(drug.drug_type) == LOWER('biologic')
).WHERE(
    # Filter for last 3 months excluding current month
    (DATEDIFF("months", start_dt, DATETIME('now')) >= 1) & 
    (DATEDIFF("months", start_dt, DATETIME('now')) <= 3)
)

# Now partition by month to count distinct patients
monthly_stats = treatments_with_month.PARTITION(
    name="months", 
    by=treatment_month
).CALCULATE(
    month = treatment_month,
    patient_count = NDISTINCT(Treatments.patient_id),
    biologic_treatment_count = NDISTINCT(Treatments.WHERE(is_biologic == True).patient_id)
)

# First, let's get treatments from the last 3 months (excluding current month)
# We'll calculate the month for each treatment and filter accordingly
treatments_with_month = Treatments.CALCULATE(
    treatment_month = JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(start_dt), LPAD(MONTH(start_dt), 2, "0")),
    patient_id = patient_id,
    is_biologic = LOWER(drug.drug_type) == LOWER('biologic')
).WHERE(
    # Filter for last 3 months excluding current month
    (DATEDIFF("months", start_dt, DATETIME('now')) >= 1) & 
    (DATEDIFF("months", start_dt, DATETIME('now')) <= 3)
)

# Now partition by month to count distinct patients
monthly_stats = treatments_with_month.PARTITION(
    name="months", 
    by=treatment_month
).CALCULATE(
    month = treatment_month,
    patient_count = NDISTINCT(Treatments.patient_id),
    biologic_treatment_count = NDISTINCT(Treatments.WHERE(is_biologic == True).patient_id)
)

# First, calculate the number of distinct drugs for each doctor
doctor_drug_counts = Doctors.CALCULATE(
    doc_id=doc_id,
    specialty=speciality,
    num_distinct_drugs=NDISTINCT(prescribed_treatments.drug_id)
)

# Then partition by specialty to enable ranking within each specialty
specialty_groups = doctor_drug_counts.PARTITION(name="specialty_groups", by=specialty)

# Finally, calculate the rank within each specialty
result = specialty_groups.Doctors.CALCULATE(
    doc_id=doc_id,
    specialty=specialty,
    num_distinct_drugs=num_distinct_drugs,
    SDRSDR=RANKING(by=num_distinct_drugs.DESC(), per="specialty_groups")
)

    specialty=speciality,

# Get treatments from the last 12 months, excluding current month
# Calculate the start of current month and go back 12 months
treatments_with_month = Treatments.WHERE(
    (start_dt >= DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-12 months')) &
    (start_dt < DATETIME('now', 'start of month'))
).CALCULATE(
    treatment_month = DATETIME(start_dt, 'start of month'),
    patient_id = patient_id,
    diag_id = diag_id
)

# Partition by month and calculate metrics
result = treatments_with_month.PARTITION(
    name="months", 
    by=treatment_month
).CALCULATE(
    month = treatment_month,
    PMPD = NDISTINCT(Treatments.CALCULATE(patient_diag = JOIN_STRINGS('-', patient_id, diag_id)).patient_diag),
    PMTC = COUNT(Treatments)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

[DEBUG] [Q77] Code generated by claude:
# First, let's get treatments with their adverse events that occurred in the same month
treatments_with_same_month_ae = Treatments.CALCULATE(
    drug_id=drug_id,
    treatment_month=MONTH(start_dt),
    treatment_year=YEAR(start_dt),
    same_month_ae_count=COUNT(
        adverse_events.WHERE(
            (MONTH(reported_dt) == MONTH(start_dt)) & 
            (YEAR(reported_dt) == YEAR(start_dt))
        )
    )
).WHERE(same_month_ae_count > 0)

# Now group by drug and sum up the adverse events
drug_ae_counts = treatments_with_same_month_ae.PARTITION(
    name="drug_groups", 
    by=drug_id
).CALCULATE(
    drug_id=drug_id,
    total_ae_count=SUM(treatments_with_same_month_ae.same_month_ae_count)
)

# Join with drug information and find the top drug
result = drug_ae_counts.TOP_K(1, by=total_ae_count.DESC()).CALCULATE(
    drug_id=drug_id,
    drug_name=Drugs.WHERE(drug_id == drug_id).SINGULAR().drug_name,
    num_adverse_events=total_ae_count
)

treatments_with_same_month_ae = Treatments.CALCULATE(
    drug_id=drug_id,
    treatment_month=MONTH(start_dt),
    treatment_year=YEAR(start_dt),
    same_month_ae_count=COUNT(
        adverse_events.WHERE(
            (MONTH(reported_dt) == MONTH(start_dt)) & 
            (YEAR(reported_dt) == YEAR(start_dt))
        )
    )
).WHERE(same_month_ae_count > 0)

# Now group by drug and sum up the adverse events
drug_ae_counts = treatments_with_same_month_ae.PARTITION(
    name="drug_groups", 
    by=drug_id
).CALCULATE(
    drug_id=drug_id,
    total_ae_count=SUM(treatments_with_same_month_ae.same_month_ae_count)
)

# Join with drug information and find the top drug
result = drug_ae_counts.TOP_K(1, by=total_ae_count.DESC()).CALCULATE(
    drug_id=drug_id,
    drug_name=Drugs.WHERE(drug_id == drug_id).SINGULAR().drug_name,
    num_adverse_events=total_ae_count
)

[DEBUG] [Q86] Code generated by claude:
# Filter treatments started in the past 6 months and get specialty info
recent_treatments = Treatments.WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("months", start_dt, DATETIME("now")) <= 6
).CALCULATE(
    doctor_specialty=doctor.speciality,
    drug_amount=tot_drug_amt
)

# Group by specialty and calculate metrics
specialty_stats = recent_treatments.PARTITION(
    name="specialties", 
    by=doctor_specialty
).CALCULATE(
    specialty=doctor_specialty,
    num_treatments=COUNT(Treatments),
    total_drug_amount=SUM(Treatments.tot_drug_amt)
)

# Get top 3 specialties by total drug amount
result = specialty_stats.TOP_K(3, by=total_drug_amount.DESC())

# Define the start date for the last 6 calendar months
six_months_ago = DATETIME('now', '-6 months')

# Filter treatments to the last 6 months and add the doctor's specialty for partitioning
treatments_with_specialty = DermTreatment.Treatments.WHERE(
    start_dt >= six_months_ago
).CALCULATE(
    doc_specialty=doctor.speciality
)

# Partition by specialty, calculate aggregates, and find the top 3
top_3_specialties_by_drug_amount = treatments_with_specialty.PARTITION(
    name="specialty_groups", by=(doc_specialty)
).CALCULATE(
    specialty=doc_specialty,
    number_of_treatments=COUNT(Treatments),
    total_drug_amount=SUM(Treatments.tot_drug_amt)
).TOP_K(3, by=total_drug_amount.DESC())

[DEBUG] [Q95] Running models for: For users in the US and Canada, how many total notifications were sent in each of the last 3 weeks excluding the current week? How many of those were sent on weekends?Weekends are Saturdays and Sundays. Truncate created_at to week for aggregation.

INFO:httpx:HTTP Request: POST https://us-east5-aiplatform.googleapis.com/v1/projects/solid-drive-448717-p8/locations/us-east5/publishers/anthropic/models/claude-opus-4@20250514:streamRawPredict "HTTP/1.1 200 OK"
[DEBUG] [Q95] Code generated by claude:
# Get notifications from US and Canada users for the last 3 weeks excluding current week
us_canada_notifications = Users.WHERE(
    (LOWER(country) == LOWER('US')) | (LOWER(country) == LOWER('Canada'))
).notifications.WHERE(
    # Filter for last 3 weeks excluding current week
    (DATEDIFF("weeks", created_at, DATETIME('now')) >= 1) & 
    (DATEDIFF("weeks", created_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 3)
).CALCULATE(
    # Truncate created_at to start of week
    week_start = DATETIME(created_at, 'start of week'),
    # Check if notification was sent on weekend (Saturday=7, Sunday=1)
    is_weekend = ISIN(DAYOFWEEK(created_at), (1, 7))
)

# Partition by week and calculate totals
result = us_canada_notifications.PARTITION(
    name="weekly_groups", 
    by=week_start
).CALCULATE(
    week_start = week_start,
    total_notifications = COUNT(Notifications),
    weekend_notifications = SUM(Notifications.is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week_start.DESC())

[DEBUG] [Q95] Code generated by gemini:
# Define the time boundaries for the last 3 weeks, excluding the current week.
# This makes the start and end dates available for the subsequent query.
ewallet_context = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    start_of_current_week=DATETIME('now', 'start of week'),
    start_of_period=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-3 weeks')
)

# Filter notifications to include only those sent within the defined time frame
# to users in the US or Canada. Then, for each notification, determine its
# corresponding week and whether it was sent on a weekend.
filtered_notifications = ewallet_context.Notifications.WHERE(
    (created_at >= start_of_period) &
    (created_at < start_of_current_week) &
    ISIN(user.country, ('US', 'Canada'))
).CALCULATE(
    notification_week=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend=ISIN(DAYNAME(created_at), ('Saturday', 'Sunday'))
)

# Partition the filtered notifications by week and calculate the total and
# weekend notification counts for each week.
weekly_notification_summary = filtered_notifications.PARTITION(
    name='weekly_groups', by=(notification_week)
).CALCULATE(
    week_start_date=notification_week,
    total_notifications=COUNT(Notifications),
    weekend_notifications=SUM(is_weekend)
)

# Get notifications from US and Canada users for the last 3 weeks excluding current week
us_canada_notifications = Users.WHERE(
    (LOWER(country) == LOWER('US')) | (LOWER(country) == LOWER('Canada'))
).notifications.CALCULATE(
    week_start = DATETIME(created_at, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend = ISIN(DAYNAME(created_at), ('Saturday', 'Sunday'))
).WHERE(
    # Filter for last 3 weeks excluding current week
    (DATEDIFF('weeks', week_start, DATETIME('now', 'start of week')) > 0) &
    (DATEDIFF('weeks', week_start, DATETIME('now', 'start of week')) <= 3)
)

# Partition by week and calculate totals
result = us_canada_notifications.PARTITION(
    name='weekly_groups', 
    by=week_start
).CALCULATE(
    week_start = week_start,
    total_notifications = COUNT(Notifications),
    weekend_notifications = SUM(Notifications.CALCULATE(weekend_flag = IFF(is_weekend, 1, 0)).weekend_flag)
).ORDER_BY(week_start.DESC())

# Define the time boundaries for the last 3 weeks, excluding the current week.
# This makes the start and end dates available for the subsequent query.
ewallet_context = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    start_of_current_week=DATETIME('now', 'start of week'),
    start_of_period=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-3 weeks')
)

# Filter notifications to include only those sent within the defined time frame
# to users in the US or Canada. Then, for each notification, determine its
# corresponding week and whether it was sent on a weekend.
filtered_notifications = ewallet_context.Notifications.WHERE(
    (created_at >= start_of_period) &
    (created_at < start_of_current_week) &
    ISIN(user.country, ('US', 'Canada'))
).CALCULATE(
    notification_week=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend=ISIN(DAYNAME(created_at), ('Saturday', 'Sunday'))
)

# Partition the filtered notifications by week and calculate the total and
# weekend notification counts for each week.
weekly_notification_summary = filtered_notifications.PARTITION(
    name='weekly_groups', by=(notification_week)
).CALCULATE(
    week_start_date=notification_week,
    total_notifications=COUNT(Notifications),
    weekend_notifications=SUM(is_weekend)
)

# Define a contextless expression to filter for wallet transactions made by US users in the last 7 days.
# The date filter DATEDIFF("days", created_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 6 correctly captures the last 7 days, including today.
transactions_in_scope = Ewallet.Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(transaction_type) == 'wallet') &
    (LOWER(sending_user.country) == 'us') &
    (DATEDIFF("days", created_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 6)
)

# Calculate the total count and sum of the amount for the filtered transactions.
wallet_transaction_summary = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    num_transactions=COUNT(transactions_in_scope),
    total_transaction_amount=SUM(transactions_in_scope.amount)
)

# Filter balances updated in the past 7 days (inclusive)
recent_balances = UserBalances.WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("days", updated_at, DATETIME("now")) <= 7
)

# Group by user and calculate average balance per user
user_avg_balances = recent_balances.PARTITION(
    name="user_groups", 
    by=(user_id,)
).CALCULATE(
    user_id=user_id,
    avg_balance=AVG(UserBalances.balance)
)

# Calculate the overall average of user averages
result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    average_balance=AVG(user_avg_balances.avg_balance)

[DEBUG] [Q98] Code generated by gemini:
# First, we create a collection of user average balances (AMB) for the specified period.
# We filter the UserBalances to include only records from the last 7 days.
# Then, we partition these records by user_id to group them for each user.
# For each user's group of balances, we calculate their average balance (AMB).
user_ambs = Ewallet.UserBalances.WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("days", updated_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 7
).PARTITION(name="user_groups", by=(user_id)).CALCULATE(
    amb=AVG(UserBalances.balance)
)

# Finally, we calculate the average of all the individual user AMBs calculated in the previous step.
# This gives the overall average AMB for users active in the last week.
average_balance_result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    average_balance=AVG(user_ambs.amb)
)

# Calculate the date range for last 2 months excluding current month
# Start of 2 months ago
start_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-2 months')
# Start of current month (exclusive end)
end_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')

# Filter transactions and calculate month
filtered_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (sender_type == 0) & 
    (created_at >= start_date) & 
    (created_at < end_date)
).CALCULATE(
    month = DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month'),
    sender_id = sender_id
)

# Partition by month and count distinct senders
result = filtered_transactions.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_groups", 
    by=month
).CALCULATE(
    month = month,
    pmdau = NDISTINCT(Transactions.sender_id)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

[DEBUG] [Q102] Code generated by gemini:
# Define the time boundaries for the last two full months.
start_of_current_month = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
start_of_period = DATETIME(start_of_current_month, '-2 months')

# Filter transactions to the specified time window and sender type, and prepare date fields for grouping.
transactions_in_range = Transactions.WHERE(
    (created_at >= start_of_period) &
    (created_at < start_of_current_month) &
    (sender_type == 0)
).CALCULATE(
    tx_month_start=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month'),
    tx_day_start=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of day')
)

# First, partition by day to calculate the Daily Active Users (DAU).
daily_active_users = transactions_in_range.PARTITION(
    name="daily_groups", 
    by=(tx_month_start, tx_day_start)
).CALCULATE(
    tx_month_start,
    dau=NDISTINCT(Transactions.sender_id)
)

# Second, partition the daily results by month to calculate the average DAU, which is the PMDAU.
pmdau_by_month = daily_active_users.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_groups", 
    by=(tx_month_start)
).CALCULATE(
    month=tx_month_start,
    pmdau=AVG(daily_groups.dau)
)

# Assign the final result, ordered by month.
result = pmdau_by_month.ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

[DEBUG] [Q107] Running models for: What was the STR for wallet transactions in the previous month?STR (success transaction rate) = number of successful transactions / total number of transactions. To determine if a transaction is successfull it's status has to be 'success'
[DEBUG] [Q107] Running model claude (attempt 0)
[DEBUG] [Q107] Running model gemini (attempt 0)
INFO:google_genai.models:AFC is enabled with max remote calls: 10.
INFO:httpx:HTTP Request: POST https://us-east5-aiplatform.googleapis.com/v1/projects/solid-drive-448717-p8/locations/us-east5/publishers/anthropic/models/claude-opus-4@20250514:streamRawPredict "HTTP/1.1 200 OK"
[DEBUG] [Q107] Code generated by claude:
# Get transactions from the previous month and calculate STR
result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    # Get the start of the previous month
    prev_month_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-1 month'),
    # Get the start of the current month (end of previous month)
    curr_month_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
).CALCULATE(
    # Filter transactions from the previous month
    prev_month_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
        (created_at >= prev_month_start) & (created_at < curr_month_start)
    ),
    # Count successful transactions (case insensitive comparison)
    successful_transactions = COUNT(Transactions.WHERE(
        (created_at >= prev_month_start) & 
        (created_at < curr_month_start) & 
        (LOWER(status) == 'success')
    )),
    # Count total transactions
    total_transactions = COUNT(Transactions.WHERE(
        (created_at >= prev_month_start) & (created_at < curr_month_start)
    ))
).CALCULATE(
    # Calculate STR as a percentage
    str_percentage = IFF(
        total_transactions > 0,
        100.0 * successful_transactions / total_transactions,
        0.0
    ),
    successful_transactions = successful_transactions,
    total_transactions = total_transactions
)

# Define the time window for the previous month
start_of_current_month = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
start_of_previous_month = DATETIME(start_of_current_month, '-1 month')

# Isolate all transactions that occurred within the previous month
transactions_in_last_month = Ewallet.Transactions.WHERE(
    (created_at >= start_of_previous_month) & (created_at < start_of_current_month)
)

# Calculate the STR by dividing successful transactions by the total number of transactions
str_previous_month = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    successful_tx_count=COUNT(transactions_in_last_month.WHERE(LOWER(status) == 'success')),
    total_tx_count=COUNT(transactions_in_last_month)
).CALCULATE(
    str_rate=IFF(total_tx_count > 0, successful_tx_count / total_tx_count, 0.0)
)

INFO:httpx:HTTP Request: POST https://us-east5-aiplatform.googleapis.com/v1/projects/solid-drive-448717-p8/locations/us-east5/publishers/anthropic/models/claude-opus-4@20250514:streamRawPredict "HTTP/1.1 200 OK"
[DEBUG] [Q110] Code generated by claude:
# First, filter active users and their successful transactions in 2023
active_users_transactions_2023 = Users.WHERE(
    LOWER(status) == LOWER('active')
).transactions_sent.WHERE(
    (YEAR(created_at) == 2023) & 
    (LOWER(status) == LOWER('successful')) &
    (sender_type == 1)  # Assuming 1 is for users, 0 for merchants based on the int32 type
).CALCULATE(
    user_id=sender_id,
    month_date=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month')
)

# Partition by month and count distinct users
monthly_active_users = active_users_transactions_2023.PARTITION(
    name="months", 
    by=month_date
).CALCULATE(
    month=month_date,
    active_users=NDISTINCT(active_users_transactions_2023.user_id)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

result = monthly_active_users

    (YEAR(created_at) == 2023) &
    (LOWER(status) == 'completed') &
    (HAS(sending_user) == 1) &
    (LOWER(sending_user.status) == 'active')
).CALCULATE(

# First, filter transactions to include only those made by active users in 2023 that were successful.
# A 'transaction_month' field is created by truncating the creation date to the start of the month.
transactions_in_2023 = Ewallet.Transactions.WHERE(
    (YEAR(created_at) == 2023) &
    (LOWER(status) == 'completed') &
    (HAS(sending_user) == 1) &
    (LOWER(sending_user.status) == 'active')
).CALCULATE(
    transaction_month=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month')
)

# Partition the filtered transactions by month.
# For each month, count the number of distinct users who sent money.
monthly_active_senders = transactions_in_2023.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_transactions",
    by=(transaction_month)
).CALCULATE(
    month=transaction_month,
    active_users_count=NDISTINCT(Transactions.sending_user.uid)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# First, filter active users and their successful transactions in 2023
active_users_transactions_2023 = Users.WHERE(
    LOWER(status) == LOWER('active')
).transactions_sent.WHERE(
    (YEAR(created_at) == 2023) & 
    (LOWER(status) == LOWER('successful')) &
    (sender_type == 1)  # Assuming 1 is for users, 0 for merchants based on the int32 type
).CALCULATE(
    user_id=sender_id,
    month_date=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month')
)

# Partition by month and count distinct users
monthly_active_users = active_users_transactions_2023.PARTITION(
    name="months", 
    by=month_date
).CALCULATE(
    month=month_date,
    active_users=NDISTINCT(active_users_transactions_2023.user_id)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

result = monthly_active_users

        (receiver_type == 1) &

    )
).CALCULATE(

).WHERE(

    total_number_of_transactions > 0
).TOP_K(
    # Finally, we select the top 2 merchants based on their total transaction amount.

# First, identify all snapshots on the earliest date in 2023.
# This is done by filtering for the year 2023, then ranking the snapshots by date in ascending order.
# Using RANKING with allow_ties=True ensures that all snapshots from the same earliest date are selected.
# We then filter for rank 1 to get only the earliest snapshots.
earliest_snapshots_in_2023 = Ewallet.UserSettingSnapshots.WHERE(
    YEAR(snapshot_date) == 2023
).CALCULATE(
    rank_by_date=RANKING(by=snapshot_date.ASC(), allow_ties=True)
).WHERE(rank_by_date == 1)

# Next, calculate the average daily and monthly transaction limits from this filtered set of snapshots.
# The final result will contain the two requested average values.
result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    avg_tx_limit_daily=AVG(earliest_snapshots_in_2023.tx_limit_daily),
    avg_tx_limit_monthly=AVG(earliest_snapshots_in_2023.tx_limit_monthly)
)

    duration_seconds=DATEDIFF("seconds", session_start_ts, session_end_ts)
)

# Partition by device_type and calculate average duration
result = sessions_with_duration.PARTITION(
    name="device_groups", 
    by=device_type
).CALCULATE(
    device_type=device_type,
    avg_duration_seconds=AVG(UserSessions.duration_seconds)
)

# For each merchant, find their earliest coupon and check if it's within a year of registration
result = Merchants.WHERE(HAS(coupons) == 1).CALCULATE(
    merchant_id=mid,
    registration_date=created_at,
    # Find the earliest coupon for this merchant
    earliest_coupon_id=coupons.WHERE(
        RANKING(by=start_date.ASC(), per="Merchants") == 1
    ).SINGULAR().cid,
    earliest_coupon_start_date=coupons.WHERE(
        RANKING(by=start_date.ASC(), per="Merchants") == 1
    ).SINGULAR().start_date
).WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("days", registration_date, earliest_coupon_start_date) <= 365
)

SELECT

  COALESCE(_s0.agg_0, 0) AS total_transactions,
  COALESCE(_s1.agg_1, 0) AS weekend_transactions

  ON _s0.week_offset = _s1.week_offset
